# CATS EIA enrichment audit report

Generated by `build/hydro_enrichment/derive_enrichment.py`. Regenerate after any change to `GIS/CATS_gens.csv`, `Archive/EIA_Generator_Y2019.csv`, or `build/generator_types.jl`; do not hand-edit.

## 1. Summary

- CATS generator rows: 3892
- Exact `(PlantCode, GenID)` matches: 2119
- Excel-serial repairs recovered: 4
- Total matched (renamed): 2123
- Unmatched: 1769 (1743 synchronous condensers + 26 imports)
- Prime movers changed: 720 of 2123 (1403 unchanged)
- Combined-cycle blocks: 65 blocks from 183 blocked units
- Hydro units: 341 over 182 plants; 93 promoted to reservoir-backed types

## 2. Renames

All 2123 matched units are renamed `gen-N` -> `<Plant Name>_<Generator ID>`. 219 names contained a comma or `#` before sanitization.

## 4. Plant grouping

One `PowerPlant` supplemental attribute per `PlantCode`. Non-CC groups are one unit per group (own shaft/penstock/PCC); combined-cycle blocks group by EIA `Unit Code` with HRSG number 1.

Plants with more than one grouped unit: 403 (single-unit plants are counted, not listed individually).

## 6. Reservoirs

Out of scope for this script (Stage 5). Reservoir derivation from NID / EHA / CDEC data is being done concurrently in a separate task; `data/hydro_units.csv` carries an empty `eha_plant_mode` column as a placeholder for that work, not a guessed value.

## 7. Gaps and guesses

- **`eha_plant_mode` left blank** in `data/hydro_units.csv` for all 341 hydro rows -- Stage 5 (external NID/EHA/CDEC data) is out of scope for this script.
- **Batteries grouped under `RenewablePowerPlant`.** The design doc's Stage 3 table does not mention `Batteries` explicitly; `plant_attribute.jl`'s `add_supplemental_attribute!` accepts `EnergyReservoirStorage` under `RenewablePowerPlant`, so the 41 raw `Batteries` rows are grouped there. This is an interpretation, not stated outright in the design doc.
- **`generator_names.csv` and `generator_prime_movers.csv` only cover the 2123 matched rows.** The 1769 unmatched rows (synchronous condensers, imports) keep their positional `gen-N` name and never receive a `prime_mover_type` in `build_CATS.jl` regardless, so no lookup row is needed for them.
- **`generator_plants.csv` excludes synchronous condensers and imports** entirely -- neither category receives a `PowerPlant` supplemental attribute.
- **Sanitization kept literal to the design doc's three rules** (strip commas, `#` -> `No`, collapse whitespace) rather than reducing names to `\w`-only characters, since the design doc's own canonical example (`Otay Mesa Generating Project_1-Jan`) keeps a hyphen and spaces.

## 8. Reservoirs (Stage 5)

Derived by `build/hydro_enrichment/derive_reservoirs.py` from `data/hydro_reservoir_sources.csv` (32 plants, NID/HILARRI/EHA/CDEC) and `data/hydro_units.csv` (promoted units). One `HydroReservoir` row per promoted plant, 32 total. Sections 1-7 above cover Stages 1-4 and are unchanged by this script.

`storage_level_min_m3` = 0.0, `powerhouse_elevation_m` = 0.0, `intake_elevation_m` = `head_m` (relative datum), `evaporative_loss` = 0.0, `inflow_m3s` = empty, and `level_data_type` = `USABLE_VOLUME` for all 32 rows -- sanctioned defaults, not measured, see 8b.

### 8b. Consolidated gaps, defaults, and guesses

- **`storage_level_min_m3` = 0.0 for all 32 rows** -- sanctioned default, not a measured empty reservoir floor.
- **`initial_level_m3` defaulted to 50% of `storage_level_max_m3` for 13 of 32 plants** (no CDEC station, or a station with no 2019-01-01 reading): Reservoir_Belden, Reservoir_Big Creek 2A, Reservoir_Big Creek 3, Reservoir_Devil Canyon, Reservoir_Electra, Reservoir_James B Black, Reservoir_Kerckhoff 2, Reservoir_Lake Hodges Hydroelectric Facility, Reservoir_Mammoth Pool, Reservoir_Parker Dam, Reservoir_Pit 4, Reservoir_Pit 5, Reservoir_Rock Creek.
- **`head_m` substituted from NID `dam_height_m` for 7 of 32 plants** (no EHA unit head available): Reservoir_Colgate, Reservoir_Devil Canyon, Reservoir_Dion R Holm, Reservoir_Lake Hodges Hydroelectric Facility, Reservoir_R C Kirkwood, Reservoir_Trinity, Reservoir_W R Gianelli.
- **`intake_elevation_m` and `powerhouse_elevation_m` are a relative datum for all 32 rows**, not absolute elevations -- no source in scope (NID, EHA plant or unit databases) carries dam or reservoir elevation at all. `powerhouse_elevation_m` = 0.0 and `intake_elevation_m` = `head_m`, so their difference reproduces the real head.
- **`turbine_type` is guessed for all 32 rows.** 2 plants (all promoted units PS) use the pumped-storage override to `FRANCIS`; the remaining 30 use the head band (>300 m PELTON, 30-300 m FRANCIS, <30 m KAPLAN) against the `head_m` value in this row (post-fallback where applicable) -- see column 8a 'turbine_type (basis)' for the head that produced each guess.
- **`evaporative_loss` = 0.0 for all 32 rows** -- sanctioned default, not a measured zero-loss reservoir.
- **`inflow_m3s` left empty for all 32 rows.** `data/hydro_reservoir_sources.csv` carries no CDEC inflow/full-natural-flow field at all (only station, capacity, 2019-01-01 storage, and 2019-01-01 elevation) -- there is nothing genuinely derivable from CDEC for any plant, not just the ones lacking a station.
- **`head_to_volume_slope` is a single linear coefficient (`head_m / storage_level_max_m3`) for the entire reservoir**, not a fitted storage-elevation curve -- CDEC's `cdec_elevation_20190101_ft` is a single point per plant (23/32 have one at all), not a curve, so a multi-point fit is not supported by the source data.
- **Known-thin reservoirs still receive full-fidelity rows**, per the design doc's accepted limitation: Pit 5 (HILARRI states outright "no reservoir", NID storage 330 acre-ft), Pit 4 (1,970 acre-ft diversion pool), Kerckhoff 2 (4,252 acre-ft forebay shared with Kerckhoff 1), and Keswick (25,132 acre-ft re-regulating basin below Shasta) are promoted by the unchanged Stage 4 capacity rule and modelled the same as Shasta's 4,661,860 acre-ft reservoir; this is unchanged by Stage 5 and not a new gap introduced here.
- **`upstream_reservoirs` (cascade topology) is out of this CSV's schema entirely** and is deferred by the design doc (refinement item 3, Big Creek and Pit River chains) -- not represented as an empty column here because the requested schema does not include it.
